# MS91 current release status

## Public shortcut review — 16 October 2026

Detector versionoriginal-accepted-finish-2026-10-16-9 retains substantial
grass-transfer evidence through flight and ambiguous intermediate road cells.
Short road grazes do not erase a pending excursion. Actual travelled distance
includes flight/ambiguous cells, preventing invented geometric distance savings.
A proven switch between distinct route branches needs sustained grass contact
and stable unique departure/re-entry, but not a half-tile grass gap. Ordinary
jumps without grass, same-arm travel, nearby corners and ambiguous endpoints
remain unproven. Original driving/finish/time physics are unchanged.

Reassessment now changes classifications without pruning any scores, public
recordings or history. Existing byte-identical proofs remain retained even when
the same driver has multiple results in a category. Optional bound score-ID
target verifies an already accepted public replay, never accepts a supplied
classification/upload, uses the global lease and validates its original identity.

Regression evidence:20 route tests,11 atomic reassessment/preservation/lease/
target tests, recorded-engine read/ranking/badge tests, TypeScript and production
build pass. Five external public recordings replayed locally: Jean de Pippel
DEFAULT1153ticks (57.65s), BERNIES Indy2003ticks (100.15s), and BERNIES P962
2144ticks (107.20s) all reproduce the original times and detectbranch_switch.
Marco legacyDEFAULT1289ticks and BERNIES2757ticks retainfull_route. Downloaded
original files are read-only test inputs outside the repository, not bundled
public source assets. No bug-fix narrative added to FAQ or README.

2026-10-13 recovered readable primary Intel manual timing evidence from the
1979 8086 Family User's Manual, Table2-21 pp2-54/2-58, hosted transcription:
https://studylib.net/doc/27791273/9800722-03-the-8086-family-users-manual-oct79
DEC reg16 is2 clocks; JNE/JNZ is16 or4 clocks. For the recovered65536-iteration
DEC DX/JNZ loop, the nominal instruction sum is65536*2+65535*16+4=1179636
CPU clocks, excluding initial MOV, prefetch/bus effects and interrupts.
This is not a fixed elapsed delay: CPU frequency and platform execution
conditions still require an explicit owning profile. No assumed ms delay or
production browser switch introduced.39 open;67/103 (65.0%),36 remaining.

## Still required before release

Hosting recovery reconnaissance: read-only Sites metadata confirms the existing
public PlayStunts site is active and owned by the current account. D1 bindingDB
contains accepted_scores, global_scores, leaderboard_reset_backups,
replay_assessment_lock, run_history, score_requests, score_tracks, shared_replays
and shared_tracks. Native table inspection truncates long replay values (the
single-row shared_replays sample explicitly reported truncation). Therefore
these inspection results are NOT a complete replay/database backup. No supported
full export/restore method is established by the inspected storage guidance or
current native tool inventory. Do not claim complete data recovery from these
rows or deploy a new public export endpoint merely to bypass this limitation.

1. Finish the source-level differences audit, including exceptional/transitive
   physics, collision and rendering branches; keep limitations explicit.
   Body-height plane logic and1120-byte MS91 checkpoint retention are now
   connected and verified against the recorded corpora and controlled crash
   cases above. Continue auditing exceptional/transitive branches and callers
   rather than treating those bounded results as an exhaustive engine proof.
2. Finish remaining original-version divider/legacy whole-passage evidence.
   Do not equate the controlled MS91 passage with original MS90 full playback.
3. Fresh browser/platform QA of the complete game, Options, updated graphics,
   sound settings, replay import/download/rewind, saves and responsive site UI.
4. Settle the complete slow-cadence release policy and fresh browser result
   gate. Private MS9110Hz exports work; normal10Hz session guards remain.
5. Verify production data/external-asset preservation and rollback readiness
   separately from the source archive, without rewriting historical records.
6. After gates pass, switch the future default to MS91, verify final-default
   behaviour, update verified FAQ/README/GitHub documentation, and publish by
   the supported workflow. Recheck the public site after deployment.

must never follow that future-race default. No remaining item is marked complete
merely because a narrower test passed.

# Roland delay counter-unit correction — 2026-10-11

Intel's original8254 datasheet confirms mode3 odd count loads count-minus-one and then decrements by two. Consequently the previously documented400/65000 are counter threshold differences, NOT elapsed PIT input clocks. Added a bounded first-high-half counter model/test: original strict-below thresholds first cross at201/32501 input clocks, excluding software polling/instruction overhead. Source: https://www.cs.usfca.edu/~cruse/cs210s07/8254.pdf . Full host still needs actual clock/polling integration; no new timing claim for browser playback. Count30/50 unchanged.

# PC15 sampled-note adapter — 2026-10-11

The note allocator now accepts an explicit sample host for slot-zero startup/replacement, forwarding versioned timer/vector requests through the previously oracle-tested sample helpers. Tests passed both driver generations, matching complete helper output/state, preserving input driver bytes and rejecting sampled startup without a host. This is not yet connected to the live allocated sound host: actual patch-pointer resolution, virtual IRQ scheduling and vector application remain required. Type checking passed. No public default or stored data change; release count remains 30/50.

# PC15 effect-sequence shutdown — 2026-10-11

Found and corrected an omitted driver-generation argument in the effect sequence's end callback. All other `stopOriginalPcSpeakerTrack` callers were checked and already forward generation. New `test_versioned_pc15_effect_end.ts` passed both layouts, verifying selected interrupt-mask/timer writes and retained-vector clearing for an inactive allocated sample owner. Type checking passed. This addresses shutdown routing, not sampled-note startup integration. Release count remains 30/50.

# PC15 resource integration — 2026-10-11

Fresh MS1991 PC-speaker startup now loads the isolated original 2227-byte PC15 driver and 1049-byte PCENG1 voice bank. Legacy startup retains catalog resources. Replay replacement keeps voice resources tied to initialized boot driver generation even when physics changes. Independent display graphs follow the live driver's generation too. `test_versioned_pc15_allocated_startup.ts` passed actual allocated startup, rendering and replay replacement for both boot generations and both replay engines, with unchanged input replay bytes and captured/silent sound output. Six component tests also passed. Sampled-effect integration and full audio semantic verification remain open; release count unchanged at 30/50.

# PC15 retained-driver routing — 2026-10-11

Allocated PC15 race and dialog adapters now select sound-state layout from the loaded driver's initialized five/seven hardware slots, independently of replay physics. Unsupported/uninitialized slot counts reject explicitly. Dialog pause/resume tests passed both generations, including all slot gains and an unchanged byte immediately outside the driver. New tests are registered in `test:pc15-versioned-memory`. Complete startup resource routing and sampled effects remain open; this does not establish full audio parity or advance the release count.

# PC15 allocated startup verification — 2026-10-11

The isolated MS1991 PC15 driver and voice resource copies passed SHA256 and copy-isolation checks (`test_versioned_pc15_resources.ts`). Allocated PC-speaker initialization/reset now accepts explicit driver generation: legacy/default retains five slots; MS1991 initializes seven slots. `test_versioned_pc15_memory_startup.ts` passed both generations and the default path, capturing output rather than emitting hardware audio. Type checking passed. This is component evidence, not completed audio integration: retained-driver generation routing, resource loading, sampled-effect integration and full gameplay audio verification remain open. Release checklist remains 30/50 verified (60%); no live change.
